# Development tables

All numbers are development; generated from results/paper_numbers.json by scripts/make_dev_tables.py; do not edit by hand.

## Provenance and conventions

- Source: `results/paper_numbers.json`, generated 2026-10-20T21:46:26+00:00, git 8f1c7e7 (dirty: False), label development.
- Bootstrap (per `meta`): 10000 resamples, seed 20261020, level 0.95, unit seed, percentile interval.
- Threshold: 0.5 kWh per 100.
- Input `f0r`: `results/f0r_dev/rows.jsonl`, 39600 rows kept of 39600, seeds [61000, 61099].
- Input `kcopy`: `results/kcopy_dev/rows.jsonl`, 43200 rows kept of 259200, seeds [61500, 61599].
- Input `scale`: `results/scale_dev/rows.jsonl`, 5040 rows kept of 5040, seeds [63000, 63019].
- Warnings in `meta`: 0.
- `value [lo, hi]` is the stored value with its stored interval. A bare value means no interval is stored. Two decimals throughout, except counts (integers) and flags (yes/no). `—` means the file does not store that key.
- Units: kWh per 100 per event unless stated. CPU in ms. E3 in units on the instance (1 unit = 0.25 kWh).

## 1. E1 by stratum

Contrast = mean gap over δ_A ∈ {2, 4} minus mean gap over δ_A ∈ {0, 1}, in kWh per 100 per event. Primary uses the best oblivious policy fixed on the tuning seeds. Floor-included lets the floor be chosen. Min-E1 uses the oblivious member that minimizes E1 itself. Oracle replaces the candidate with oracle_mpc.

## 4. E3 summary and pointwise misses

E3 summary fields are stored in `numbers` under `e3/summary/*`, not in `meta`. Simultaneous passes use the Bonferroni 95% interval, which is the primary rule. Pointwise misses use the percentile interval for the measured value.

| Summary | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Points | 312 |
| Simultaneous passes (primary rule: Bonferroni 95% interval) | 312 |
| Simultaneous failures | 0 |
| Pointwise misses (predicted outside the percentile interval) | 11 |
| Expected failures by chance | 15.60 |
| Binomial p (upper) | 0.91 |

| R_pop | 144 | 144 | 139 | 5 |
| gap_oracle | 144 | 144 | 141 | 3 |
| gap_e1 | 24 | 24 | 21 | 3 |

## 6. Floor trade-off, return-probability sweep, lag split and RWDF-E

### 6a. Floor trade-off

Event shortfall is the floor's mean shortfall (kWh per 100 per event). Net recovery gain is R_avoid(best oblivious) − R_avoid(floor). The two are reported side by side and are never summed.
